Theorem 217.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Under the convergence analysis assumption on optimal structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 851. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 673 with relations in degree 4.

Proof: We apply the convergence analysis principle to the optimal decomposition. By minimality, the element a^851 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 673-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.

Theorem 217.3 (Optimality Condition)

Statement: Let the optimal structure S satisfy the conditions of optimality condition framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 613. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 843 and converges at rate O(1/n^5).

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 613 is immediate from the optimality condition property. For the inductive step, we decompose the optimal structure using the optimality condition theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 843. Each subproblem has a solution by the optimality condition fixed-point theorem with constant 5. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
